NCERT solutions for English - Honeydew Class 8 3.2 Macavity : The Mystery Cat Working with the Poem [Pages 51 - 52]
Read the first stanza and think.
(i) Is Macavity a cat really?
(ii) If not, who can Macavity be?
Complete the following sentence.
A master criminal is one who _____________________.
Complete the following sentence.
The Scotland Yard is baffled because_______________.
Complete the following sentence.
________because Macavity moves much faster than them.
“A cat, I am sure, could walk on a cloud without coming through”. (Jules Verne)
Which law is Macavity breaking in the light of the comment above?
Read stanza 3, and then, describe Macavity in two or three sentences of your own.
Say ‘False’ or ‘True’ for the following statement.
Macavity is not an ordinary cat.
True
False
Say ‘False’ or ‘True’ for the following statement.
Macavity cannot do what a fakir can easily do.
True
False
Say ‘False’ or ‘True’ for the following statement.
Macavity has supernatural powers.
True
False
Say ‘False’ or ‘True’ for the following statement.
Macavity is well-dressed, smart, and bright.
True
False
Say ‘False’ or ‘True’ for the following statement.
Macavity is a spy, a trickster and a criminal, all rolled in one.
True
False
Having read the poem, try to guess whether the poet is fond of cats. If so, why does he call Macavity a fiend and monster?
Has the poet used exaggeration for special effects? Find a few examples of it and read those lines aloud.

Streams
Levels
Advertisements
Range/Scope of Entrance examination
Choosing a course is easy, but getting into it requires passing an entrance exam. Colleges and institutes provide admission into courses such as diploma, post diploma, under graduate, post graduate, research and fellowship programmes based on the score and ranking obtained by the candidate in the entrance examination. Even for admission into all types of professional courses, an entrance examination is required. The entrance exam has a wide range of applications because it provides the opportunity to build a solid educational foundation in today's competitive market.
The primary goal of administering an entrance exam is to assess a student's ability, sharpness, knowledge, and so on. In the entrance exam, the student's aptitude is tested. The entrance exam follows a specific pattern; after the students complete the written test, the shortlisted candidates in the written test are followed by the group discussion round and the personal interview.
Advertisements
Kinds of Entrance Examinations in India
Engineering entrance exams, Fashion and technology entrance exams, Film and television entrance exams, Management entrance exams, Medical entrance exams, Science/computer entrance exams, Law entrance exams, and so on are the most popular entrance exams among students.
Following is a list of most preferred entrance / Competitive exams- National Eligibility cum Entrance Exam for Medicine
- Joint Entrance Examination (JEE) for Engineering
- All India Exam for Agriculture
- National Council for Hotel Management Joint Entrance Examination
- Common Law Admission Test
- IISERs BS-MS Admission Exam
- Graduate Aptitude Test in Engineering (GATE)
- National Aptitude Test in Architecture (NATA)
- State Engineering Entrance Exam.
Advertisements
Competitive Exams after 12th (Science, Commerce, and Arts)
Students nowadays have the opportunity to pursue a course in their field of interest after completing their 12th grade. After high school, students can pursue unconventional careers such as social work, liberal arts, sociology, design, and architecture. Passing an entrance exam after 12th grade will allow you to select the degree of your choice at the UG level.
Students studying in any of the three streams (Science, Commerce, and Arts) have a variety of options in Competitive after 12th. They can gain admission to their desired course and a scholarship for further study by passing an entrance exam after their 12th grade. These recent high school graduates have the potential to contribute to the country's various sectors of the economy. Following the 12th grade, there are specific entrance exams for commerce, science (PCM/Biology), and arts. After the 12th grade, students can pursue careers in engineering, management, law, medicine, architecture, and a variety of other popular fields.
Advertisements
"What Entrance Exam Should I Take After 12th?" is a common dilemma that every student faces at some point in their lives. In light of the current situation, we can safely say that the days of doctors, engineers, accountants, and teachers among the top professions are long gone. Students today have a variety of traditional and unconventional career options after graduating from high school. Students can sit for entrance exams after completing their 12th grade to gain admission to their desired UG programme at top-ranked universities that offer cutting-edge courses. There are many more options available in government jobs for which they can sit for competitive exams.
Entrance examinations are used to gain admission to a variety of undergraduate, graduate, and professional degree programmes. Generally, entrance examinations are held by educational institutes and colleges at the higher level of education. After passing the entrance exam, students can apply for admission to the specialised degree programme of their choice.

Privacy Policy
We value your trust. In order to honour that trust, Shaalaa.com adheres to ethical standards in gathering, using, and safeguarding any information you provide. Shaalaa.com is a edtech company, incorporated in India, for imparting learning. This privacy policy governs your use of the Shaalaa.com App ('Application') and www.shaalaa.com ('Website') and the other associated applications, products, websites and services managed by the Company. Please read this privacy policy ('Policy') carefully before using the Application, Website, our services and products, along with the Terms of Use ('ToU') provided on the Application and the Website. Your use of the Website, Application, or services in connection with the Application, Website or products ('Services'), or registrations with us through any modes or usage of any products including through tablets or other storage/transmitting device shall signify your acceptance of this Policy and your agreement to be legally bound by the same. If you do not agree with the terms of this Policy, do not use the Website, Application our products or avail any of our Services.

The Application/Website/Services/products obtains the information you provide when you register for the Application or Services or products. When you register with us, you generally provide (a) your name, age, email address, location, phone number, password and your ward's educational interests; (b) transaction-related information, such as when you make purchases, respond to any offers, or use applications from us; (c) information you provide us when you contact us for help; (d) information you enter into our system when using the Application/Services/products, such as while asking doubts, participating in discussions and taking tests. The said information collected from the users could be categorized as "Personal Information", "Sensitive Personal Information" and "Associated Information". Personal Information, Sensitive Personal Information and Associated Information (each as individually defined under this Information Technology (Reasonable security practices and procedures and sensitive personal data or information) Rules, 2011 (the "Data Protection Rules")) shall collectively be referred to as 'Information' in this Policy. We may use the Information to contact you from time to time, to provide you with the Services, important information, required notices and marketing promotions. We will ask you when we need more information that personally identifies you (personal information) or allows us to contact you. We will not differentiate between who is using the device to access the Application, Website or Services or products, so long as the log in/access credentials match with yours. Frequently asked questions about NCERT Solutions for Social Science - Resources and Development [English] Class 8
What is Geography?
Geography studies different places and the relationships and bonds between people and their environment. Geographers are involved in exploring the Earth’s physical properties, and they conduct in-depth studies of the human societies that are thriving across the globe. Geography is also about learning how places and locations affect people and human culture. Geography is about understanding precisely where specific things are found, why they are found there, and how they develop and keep changing over time. Learn various Geography concepts with the help of NCERT Solutions Our Past 3 Part 1 Class 8 to better understand the entire syllabus. It is the best time to prepare for the upcoming CBSE examinations.
Why should you study Geography?
Geography should be studied to create an awareness of a specific place. A thorough understanding of Geography helps instil identity in a nation like India, the United States, etc. Geography helps you to learn the names of various cities and precisely where they are located. You get to know your neighbouring countries. Geography is taught as a part of Social Science in CBSE. You may consider gaining an in-depth knowledge of Geography by reading and practising the answers given in NCERT Books Solutions For Class 8 Social Science.
Studying Geography helps students to understand the historical and present-day events and incidents of political, social, and economic importance. You can become much better critical thinkers by studying Geography and processing all this valuable information. We cannot deny the fact that Geography is very much an integral part of our lives. You may also go through NCERT Resources and Development Class 8 to build confidence and secure high exam marks.
Why should you opt for NCERT Solutions Geography Class 8?

The Maharashtra government established the Maharashtra State Bureau of Textbook Production and Curriculum Research (Balbharati) on January 27, 1967, in order to improve the standard and affordability of MSBSHSE textbooks.

Frequently asked questions about Balbharati solutions
Why is studying from Balbharati important?
For boards like the Maharashtra State Board, which refers to the Balbharati Textbook, studying from these books can help obtain grades in subjects like Maths, Science, Social Science, English, Hindi, and Marathi.
What are the Subjects in Social Sciences by Balbharati?
Social Science has subjects like History, Civics and Geography
Where can I download eBalbharati solved practice sets?
At Shaalaa, our experts have solved various eBalbharati practice sets. These sets are available on the Shaalaa website and app.
What's the difference between the Maharashtra state board and Balbharati?
The Maharashtra State Board is the board that runs the course. Balbharati is the book that the Maharashtra State Board refers to. Maharashtra State Board Solutions and Balbharati Solutions for Textbooks are the same thing.

While you are preparing for most exams, it is essential to complete your syllabus. Another vital aspect is effectively utilizing your time. Exam preparation should begin earlier so that you always get a chance to revise your studies.
Start to Study Early: People can only study part of their portion a day before their examination. Finishing the entire syllabus at least a few weeks before the examination is essential and will give us time to revise the study material. Reading the Balbharati textbook can help you a lot.
Organize yourself: You'll need to organize so that you know the portion that needs to be covered daily. Planning how many days you have to finish your studies is crucial. So that you have enough time to revise, solving questions can go a long way.

The Balbharati solution guide can help students during their regular studies. Now, you can refer to solutions on Shaalaa to understand how to structure an answer appropriately.

Timing yourself while answering is a great practice. It helps to understand that you give the correct time for every question and section.
Remember that being well-hydrated is essential for your body and brain to work at their best. Make sure you keep drinking plenty of water throughout, whether it is your revision or your exam day.
Parents, brothers, and sisters don't have to be annoying around exam time. Use them to your advantage. If you cannot explain it to them, you will know it's time to go back and understand the question yourself.

Frequently asked questions about Maharashtra State Board study material
In Maharashtra State Board, what is the full form of SSC?
In Maharashtra State Board, SSC is a Secondary School Certificate, which means a student has completed the 10th Standard.
In Maharashtra State Board, what is the full form of HSC?
In India, there are currently 52 approved boards serving 30 states. The majority of educational institutions are connected to the state boards. The HSC acronym refers to the Higher Secondary Board, which spans two years between classes XI and XII and culminates in the HSC test. Science, commerce, and the arts are the three streams. Aside from that, students can choose from various cross-disciplinary options, such as biology, pure science, and numerous commerce disciplines. The HSC exams occur every March, and students must score 40 per cent in the five leading courses out of six to pass. Students are given grades based on their percentage of marks out of a possible 100. Practical papers in science courses are of Max 30 marks.
Where can I find Maharashtra Board Solutions?
Maharashtra Board Past Years Question Papers Solutions and Textbook Solutions can be found on Shaalaa.com.
Where can I download the MSBSHSE 10th Question Paper?
You can download the MSBSHSE 10th Question Paper from the above links.
How can I download Maharashtra state textbooks?
Maharashtra State Board Balbharati textbooks for 1st, 2nd, 3rd, 4th, 5th, 6th, 7th, 8th, 9th, 10th, 11th, and 12th have been uploaded online.
The Solutions for the Maharashtra State Board are available at this link.

Frequently asked questions
What are the benefits of solving the previous years’ question papers?
When students solve the question papers from prior years, they build confidence and obtain a sense of the marking policy and the kinds of questions that may be given in the Board exam.
How can class 10 students CBSE prepare for challenging topics like math and science?
Students must continuously practise and become accustomed to the kinds of questions that will be offered on the test. This will increase their self-assurance and enable them to do better on tests.
Are the questions from the last ten years' exam papers sufficient?
The past ten years' exam questions papers should be solved because they are likely to be repeated. Additionally, the CBSE syllabus could change, making the preceding ten years' problems more pertinent.

On Shaalaa.com, all the textbook solutions are available for free. You'll be able to read and learn from the answers provided here. Shaalaa is the largest source of textbook solutions.
What are the benefits of textbook solutions?
Textbook answers are a great resource for helping with homework and preparing for tests and exams. When students study for tests, the textbook solution's question structure is helpful. Students can obtain practical, adaptable, and simple-to-understand knowledge with the help of expert textbook solutions.
What website gives you answers to textbooks?
Shaalaa.com provides millions of detailed, step-by-step answers to every question in the most widely used middle school, high school, and college textbooks-answers to math, science, history, economics, and more.

Maharashtra SSC, HSC Board Exam question banks have been made available to students of classes 10 and 12. Maharashtra State Council of Educational Research and Training has released the subject-wise question banks for the Maharashtra Board Exams. The Question bank includes multiple questions and can be accessed online.
A subject-wise question bank for the upcoming Maharashtra class 10 and 12 annual Board Examinations has been released by the Maharashtra State Council for Educational Research and Training. You can download the English question papers for Maharashtra Class 10 History and Political Science. For mathematics papers parts 1, 2, and 3, and geography papers, you can download the Marathi question papers. For the class 12 Arts, Commerce, and Science streams, you can download the Mathematics and Statistics, Physics, Chemistry, Biology, English, and History subject question banks.

Maharashtra state board has released the Maharashtra Board SSC and HSC Board Exam Question Bank for the students appearing for the annual board examinations. The Maharashtra State Council of Educational Research and Training has released the subject-wise question banks for the upcoming Maharashtra class 10 and 12 Annual Board examinations. Students can download the question banks containing multiple questions from the official website of the Maharashtra Academic Authority.
Class 10 History and Political Science question banks are available in English, while Maths Part 1 and 2, Geography subjects are available in Marathi. As for HSC or Class 12, the question banks for Arts, Commerce and Science students' Subjects are Mathematics and statistics for Arts, Science and Maths, Physics, Chemistry, Biology, English, and History subjects are now available.
